## R50-1 — command arrays cannot launch the proposed checks correctly

**P2, preparation defect.** [gate-proposal.json:43](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/Artifacts/support-v2/candidate/gate-proposal.json:43) and line55 split `-Project=`, the workspace, and `/ShibuyaUnreal.uproject` into three arguments. UBT needs the project option and complete project filename as one argument. [Line75](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/Artifacts/support-v2/candidate/gate-proposal.json:75) instead puts the complete camera command in one `arguments` element. Passing that array through normal Windows argv quoting makes the whole project-plus-options text one argument. Treating it as raw text leaves the space-bearing `-ExecCmds` and `-TestExit` values without a defined quoting contract.

Required correction: freeze one real argv representation for each launcher. Each UBT command must contain one complete `-Project=<absolute project file>` argument. Camera argv must separate project, Entry map and flags, while retaining each full ExecCmds/TestExit value in one argument. Bind the actual Windows command-line serialization, executable, working directory, environment, SDK/library/header inputs and owned launcher before dispatch. Verify the actual report contains exactly the two named camera tests, rather than interpreting a successful process exit or zero discovered tests as a pass. These are command-preparation defects; they are not observed compile or consumer failures.

## R50-2 — the prepared 61-case population leaves required paths unexercised

**P2, focused-gate preparation gap.** [prepare_schema2_probe.py:65](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/tools/runtime/prepare_schema2_probe.py:65) creates no road base outputs, and lines67/81 leave photographic, raw and native roads empty in every case. Consequently no planned case enters the new road binding/identity loop at [SupportSchema2Admission.h:188](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/Source/ShibuyaRuntime/Public/SupportSchema2Admission.h:188). The complete case list at lines107–161 also has no reparse/ancestor case, unknown or conflicting fixed-role case, or extreme finite cancellation case for the newly written expansion determinant. The subnormal positive is useful, but its nonzero determinant follows the fast sign path; it does not demonstrate recovery when rounded double differences lose the determinant. The simple exact-zero fixtures exercise a much narrower fallback.

Required correction: add a nonempty valid raw/photo/native road fixture and focused altered source/index/actor/mesh role negatives; actual owned leaf and ancestor reparse refusals through unchanged RuntimeFiles; fixed-role conflict/unknown-role refusals; and independent finite binary-float determinant controls that distinguish exact zero from cancellation-hidden nonzero. For example, finite F32 ENU points `A=(2^100,2^100,0)`, `B=(2^-100,0,0)`, `C=(0,2^-100,0)` have exact XY determinant `-2+2^-200`, while naïvely rounded double differences yield zero. This is a proposed algebraic control, not an executed result. Use independently specified expected signs/predicates, preserve the tiny nonzero positive, and add a valid extreme exact-zero counterpart. Mutated ledger/GLB bytes must be rebound so the intended helper is reached.

At [prepare_schema2_probe.py:90](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/tools/runtime/prepare_schema2_probe.py:90), the default required diagnostic is empty; only the missing-current and wrong-original cases set it. Thus 57 of the 59 prepared negatives have no failure-specific diagnostic expectation, despite the gate proposal requiring one. Bind each negative to its intended diagnostic or an equally precise reached-branch witness and freeze the independent expected case population. A rejection for an unrelated earlier error must not certify the named predicate. The two positive cases still remain necessary. No case was generated or run by this reviewer, and no prepared negative is counted as an observed red gate.

## R50-3 — preparation reads and writes do not enforce the branch path rule

**P2, before preparation dispatch.** [prepare_schema2_probe.py:89](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/tools/runtime/prepare_schema2_probe.py:89) creates the caller-supplied destination without confining it to the task's ignored output root or inspecting existing ancestors. Lines98–106 and174–178 write through ordinary pathlib operations; lines166–172 read source, baseline and template the same way. `Path.resolve()` at line14 follows links before any inspection. [generate_support_deployment.ps1:4](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/tools/runtime/generate_support_deployment.ps1:4) and lines30–34 similarly read metadata and read/overwrite the target header without reparse inspection. An existing junction in the destination ancestry or reparse target header can redirect these operations outside the intended owned locations. `exist_ok=False` only refuses an existing destination leaf; it does not reject a redirected ancestor.

Required correction: add lexical confinement and fail-closed inspection of every existing ancestor and leaf before these operations, preserving explicit fixed source/baseline/template roles and the fresh ignored destination. Alternatively, freeze and review an actual enclosing preparation launcher that supplies and enforces the entire path contract for these entry points; the present proposal's future ownership paragraph does not establish that file admission. Include owned negative path controls and exact positive output readback. No real redirected read/write or outside-file modification was attempted. The production RuntimeFiles guard remains unchanged and is not the defect identified here.

### Findings requiring repair before dispatch

**R50-4 — P2: enforce actual project and local include membership before compiling.** At [run_support_owned.py:57](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/tools/runtime/run_support_owned.py:57), the wrapper verifies only listed candidate files. Lines60–69 enforce directory membership only for the 13 external console header roots. No equivalent guard covers project Source or project-local include search directories. UBT discovers additional translation units under its module directory without a change to .uproject or ShibuyaRuntime.Build.cs. An extra Source/ShibuyaRuntime/Private/Unexpected.cpp therefore leaves every frozen hash unchanged and is compiled into Editor/Game. The pinned project declares the ShibuyaRuntime module; the pinned Build.cs does not restrict that source discovery.

The console path has a related hole. The response file adds Source/Public and the candidate toolchain directory before Engine include roots, and quoted includes also search the including source file's directory. An extra CoreMinimal.h beside generated schema2_probe.cpp, in Source/Public, or beside RuntimeFiles.cpp can alter compilation while all frozen file pins and all 5,179 external header pins stay green. The prepared-output capture at lines161–166 records files present at preparation, but prerequisite checks at lines84–86 do not refuse a later added header. Production World currently includes the exact schema2 helper at line171 and calls AdmitSupportDeployment at line266, which calls AdmitSupportSchema2 at header line269. The required symbols are present in the inspected bytes; later compilation is not constrained to those inspected inputs.

Required repair: freeze and check complete applicable Source/target/Build.cs membership and local console include/search memberships before and after the relevant stages, retaining lexical confinement and no-reparse checks. Keep generated probe source and permitted compiler outputs as distinct exact stage populations so compiler-created .obj/.exe files do not require broad exclusions or deletion. Bind the actual .uproject, module/target rules, required entry points, explicit /FI files and response/include order to the freeze. If the actual project can discover additional local plugin/module roots, constrain their membership or expected absence too. Do not delete unrelated output or relax external header membership.

Required negative controls, still UNRUN: in fresh task-owned fixtures, an added module .cpp must fail membership preflight before UBT starts; a shadow header in each applicable local search-root class must fail before cl.exe starts; a removed or changed required source/header must fail by its exact role. The unchanged approved population must pass. Record the failing path and expected population digest. Do not inject files into another worker's active tree.

**R50-5 — P2: use complete stage-specific semantic populations and measured total budgets.** [run_support_owned.py:60](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/tools/runtime/run_support_owned.py:60) reads every external record and enumerates every external header root before selecting a stage; line174 repeats this after every stage. The external population is 1,257,561,445 bytes: 825 compiler/linker/UBT-runtime records (901,691,206 bytes), 5,179 console-header records (303,501,994), 18 installed-engine-admission records (30,589,422), three command executables (745,600), 152 portable SDK records (20,943,905), and the SDK manifest (89,318). Eight stages incur two full passes each: 20,120,983,120 external bytes across the sequence, excluding candidate inputs and generated prerequisites. Pure syntax/fixture/path/generator verdicts cannot depend on UBT or C++ headers; console execution does not compile headers.

The independently measured 47.144-second pass establishes material critical-path work, without predicting the candidate wrapper's duration. The inner timer starts only at line97, after its first full pass and prerequisite hashing. The outer timer starts after its own imports/current-input verification at [run_support_outer.py:39](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/tools/runtime/run_support_outer.py:39). Inner reported duration omits initial conservation cost, and no separate conservation/enumeration timing is recorded. The outer watchdog allows only stage.seconds + 60 for the entire inner process, including both full inventory passes, without exposing that cost separately.

Required repair: freeze independently reviewed input subsets per stage, covering all actual semantic dependencies, exact membership where discovery occurs, before/after conservation and receipt binding to each subset's digest. Measure initial reads/hashes, membership enumeration, prerequisite/output hashing, command work, physical cleanup and final census separately, plus total elapsed time from entry. Give the enclosing watchdog a finite budget for all parts. Reusing an immutable upstream certificate requires R50-6's exact dependency identity/output binding; simply skipping relevant conservation is not a repair.

### Material findings

**R50-7 — P2: use the same file-order rule in the final census as the producer.** [support_gate_contract.py:78](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/tools/runtime/support_gate_contract.py:78) returns Python-sorted directory and file lists. [census_support.ps1:72](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/tools/runtime/census_support.ps1:72) sorts actual members using Sort-Object -CaseSensitive before comparing those lists. Case-sensitive culture sorting is not ordinal sorting. The script already defines an Ordinal-Sorted helper at line29 but never calls it.

Each generated case contains both an uppercase ShibuyaUnreal.uproject member and lowercase data/... members. Python orders the former before the latter within that case; the current PowerShell sort places data first. A literal read-only observation of the two names valid-schema2-with-tiny-nonzero-face/ShibuyaUnreal.uproject and valid-schema2-with-tiny-nonzero-face/data/case.json returned data first. That observation executed no candidate function, created no fixture, and is not a candidate RED result. It is sufficient to establish that an otherwise successful prepare stage will fail the census's whole-output-file-population comparison. Other mixed-case or punctuation paths can differ too.

Required repair: adopt one explicit case-sensitive ordering/equality convention for all file and directory populations, including duplicate handling, across Python and PowerShell. Use the existing ordinal helper where it supplies that convention, or compare exact unique populations without relying on culture-dependent order. Do not weaken the membership check or drop either expected entry. Add a focused control with these actual case path forms, mixed case and punctuation so an equal population passes and a missing/extra member still fails. Refreeze the census and its dependent manifests. The first prepare stage should not be leased under the present bytes.

**R50-4 remains P2 for generated UBT include/search roots.** The authored Source24/project, Source/Public/Private, console forced-header directory, generated standalone probe source, external include populations and absent project Plugins/Mods/Platforms are now constrained. This resolves the original added-Source-.cpp and standalone-console shadow-header examples in code. Exact required live symbols, module dependencies and target types are also checked.

The remaining hole is [support_gate_contract.py:249](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/tools/runtime/support_gate_contract.py:249): compile_readback enumerates every file under each generated_input_roots directory and adds every discovered name to the trusted known set. It does this after UBT has run. The Editor roots include the whole Intermediate/Build/Win64/x64/ShibuyaUnrealEditor subtree; Game similarly includes ShibuyaUnreal. The frozen actual shared response explicitly adds the target's Development/UnrealEd or Development/Engine directory as an include root, and the per-object responses force-include a SharedPCH header from that directory. The module intermediate directory also contains forced definitions and generated sources.

No preflight discovery or required-absence contract constrains those generated target/module directories. The only exact generated output population in each target stage is its six-file UHT directory. Consequently, a pre-existing extra header in the target PCH include directory can participate in the actual include or quoted forced-header search, appear in the compiler dependency JSON, and be accepted simply because the post-build enumeration found it. Capturing the resulting entire target tree as output records the additional file but does not establish that it was an approved compiler input. The current absence of Intermediate is an observation, not an enforced launch condition.

Required repair: constrain the actual generated search roots as inputs, with a fresh/expected-absent precondition where appropriate and an independently declared allowed generated source/header/forced-include population. Bind legitimate generated bytes to their producing stage and frozen project/rules before admitting them for compilation; do not automatically promote every file from a broad post-build subtree into known inputs. Compiler outputs can have a separate output contract. Preserve the existing Source, response-order and dependency-readback checks. Limit cleanup or fresh-root handling to the task-owned target/module directories; do not delete a broad shared Intermediate tree.

Required focused controls, UNRUN: add a shadow header to a task-owned analogue of the actual UBT target PCH include root and to the generated module/forced-definition search context; each must refuse before child compilation. An unexpected compiler-reported generated include must also fail rather than becoming trusted by directory membership. The legitimate generated population must pass. The existing shadow-generated-probe control covers the standalone probe directory and does not cover these UBT roots.

**R50-8 — P2: bind module selection in the separate PowerShell census process.** The runtime/census manifests pin the bundled PowerShell deployment and CimCmdlets module. The plan sets PSModulePath to the bundled Modules directory for commands launched inside the inner wrapper. [run_support_owned.py:99](C:/Users/38909/Documents/github/maps/artifacts/unreal-support-v2/worktree/tools/runtime/run_support_owned.py:99) applies that environment only in its own process and restores it at lines161–163. This cannot set the parent process's environment for the separately launched post-exit census.

The frozen census invocation in dispatch-proposals.json is pwsh -NoLogo -NoProfile -NonInteractive -File census_support.ps1 with stage/inventory arguments. It supplies no process-local module environment. The census script never sets PSModulePath or explicitly imports the pinned module by absolute path before its unqualified Get-CimInstance at line84. NoProfile suppresses profiles; it does not by itself constrain module discovery to the bundled files whose hashes were verified. An inherited module search path can select a different CimCmdlets implementation while the pinned bundled files remain unchanged. Thus the claimed complete census dependency binding is missing an actual selection condition.

Required repair: make the separate census launch contract explicitly enforce the approved process-local module path before module autoload, or load the exact pinned bundled modules by absolute path with selection verification, and bind that contract in the frozen invocation/receipt. Preserve normal system state; no global environment or installed-module mutation is needed. Record the actual Get-CimInstance module/assembly identity and require it to match the pinned dependency. A focused owned control should show that an alternative module on an inherited search path cannot be selected, while the approved bundled selection succeeds. Do not equate hashing the intended module with observing which module executed.

**R50-13 — P1: the no-late-error call is converted to the wrong validation branch.** `r50-v7-envelope-controls/controls.ps1:30` declares `ExactDiagnostic(...,[string]$Late)`. Line 32 tests `$null -eq $Late`, while the absent-writer arm passes `$null` at line 77. PowerShell's string parameter conversion makes that argument an empty string. The validator therefore expects one receipt error and an encoded empty string, while the correct absent-writer diagnostic contains zero receipt errors and `<none>`. The fifth ordered control must refuse even with a correct shared finalizer, before all eight rows can be completed. The same null call shape appears in the missing-original negative at line 87 and can make that negative reject for an incidental branch mismatch. Preserve actual null at this boundary using an appropriate untyped/object parameter or an explicit independently checked presence discriminator. Keep a real empty error string distinct from absence. Re-review the corrected branch and its two call sites; the next authorized eight-control run must reach all eight intended outcomes, not just an exit/refusal.

**R50-14 — P1: before-proof refusal still calls every real environment setter.** In `candidate-v7/prepare-environment.ps1:82–90`, originals are captured before writing/flushing/disposing/pinning the before-proof. Any open, serialization, write, flush, disposal or pin failure can reach catch with `$before` populated, `before_written=false` and `mutation_started=false`. Finally at lines 102–104 nevertheless calls `RestoreSelected $before` unconditionally. That helper, lines 30–33, calls the actual Process setter for every row. Thus a failed durable before-proof does not prevent environment setter calls; the diagnostic may still report that mutation never started. Assigning the captured values back is still a write and is unnecessary before the first selected assignment. Gate setter-based restoration on whether assignment actually began; on the no-mutation path, retain safe readback and independent error/receipt reporting without setters. Once assignment begins, preserve the existing attempt-every-key behavior despite any restoration error.

The two existing missing-before/write-failure arms currently invoke only `RequireBeforeProof` and `FinishReceipt` (`controls.ps1:47–51`), so they do not exercise this envelope orchestration defect. As Root directed during this review, minimally expose the actual selected-row orchestration and make those same two arms exercise it with only the existing three synthetic Process keys: missing/unwritten/unpinned before-proof must result in zero assignment/restore setters and zero child dispatch, while assignment-started failure must still attempt every selected restoration. Preserve the ordered eight population and the fixed native key/command population; no native-key experiment or extra gate is requested.

**R50-15 — P2: the two “literal” negatives are built by the serializer being checked.** `controls.ps1:83–87` constructs a Context, calls the selected candidate `DiagnosticLine`, then removes the original or late-error field. These are serializer-derived fixtures, not complete immutable diagnostic literals independent of that implementation. A changed serializer can alter the malformed fixture and its rejection reason together, defeating the promised independent old-output diagnostic check. Replace these two fixtures with fixed complete 27-field negative records whose error encodings and nonbinding fields do not come from `DiagnosticLine` or selected `Encoded`; substitute only the frozen source/inventory bindings where necessary. Keep the same two names and exact intended parser refusal. The six positive runtime diagnostics already have separate Python field/message expectations; this finding is limited to the claimed literal negative independence, not a claim that the whole suite self-validates.

### R50-24 — P1: checkpoint metadata uses code-point order where both consumers require UTF-16 ordinal order

The pinned decision-authority.json evidence array first puts the path ending census-controls/ps-ordinal-case-substitution-refused/\uE000.json at line 6465 before the path ending /\uD800\uDC00.json at line 6471. These represent U+E000 and U+10000. U+10000 starts with UTF-16 unit D800, which precedes E000, so the required ordinal order is the reverse. The same inversion occurs in ten retained fixture directories. Reviewer-owned literal metadata comparison established this discrepancy; this was not a candidate check execution.

support_gate_contract.py:267–268 requires the complete 358 evidence paths to equal its unique UTF-16 ordinal ordering and raises “Fixed v11 decision complete evidence population mismatch” otherwise. census_support.ps1:141 applies Require-CensusOrderedIdentities to the same array. The outer wrapper calls decision_checkpoint at run_support_outer.py:37 before creating its child; the inner calls it at run_support_owned.py:68 before commands. Thus this exact freeze cannot reach deterministic-check.

The same ten inversions also appear in decision-authority.json whole_run_population.files, beginning at lines 8845–8846, and in final-conservation.json accepted_run_population.files. Fixing evidence alone would leave a second refusal: support_gate_contract.py:270–271 captures the actual ordinal population and requires both arrays canonical; census_support.ps1:142 compares the actual ordinal capture with the pinned expected array. The 255-directory array is already correctly ordered.

Required repair: preserve every member, byte count, hash, cap and original v11 artifact; rebuild the new authority evidence paths and whole-run files with explicit UTF-16 code-unit ordinal ordering, keeping exact uniqueness. Update the copied new conservation expectation and all enclosing v12 pins. Verify both arrays independently against the existing ordinal contract, including all ten U+10000/U+E000 pairs. Preserve the rejected freeze as evidence. Do not change the consumers, downgrade ordered equality to set equality, remove the beyond-BMP files, reorder the immutable v11 receipts or alter the three commands. This is an order-only metadata repair and needs focused static re-review before any lease.
